                                                         Chairman’s Statement

2019年 統 一 企 業 中 國 控 股 有 限 公 司（「本 公    In 2019, Uni-President China Holdings Ltd. (the “Company”) and
司」）及 其 附 屬 公 司（ 統 稱「本 集 團」或「我         its subsidiaries (together as the “Group”, “our Group”, “we”, “our”
們」）秉 持「誠 實 勤 道、創 新 求 進」的 經 營 理        or “us”) adhered to the corporate vision of “Honesty and Diligence;
念，堅 持「價 值 營 銷」，聚 焦 經 營，致 力 打 造        Innovation and Excellence（ 誠 實 勤 道、創 新 求 進）” and upheld
品牌價值，穩健踏實走自己的路，以「健康操                  the principle of “Value Marketing（ 價值營銷）”. With a focus on
作」循 序 漸 進 追 求 進 步，致 力 成 為 一 個 具 備     operation, we were dedicated to creating brand value, pursuing
「品格」、「品牌」與「品味」的三品企業。                  steady development and striving for progressive advancement
                                      by “Healthy Practice（ 健康操作）”, with an aim to evolve into an
                                      enterprise boasting “Integrity（ 品格）”, “Brand（ 品牌）” and “Taste
                                      （ 品味）”.


2019年受到中美貿易協商的不確定影響，眾                 In 2019, subject to the uncertainties of the Sino-US trade
多 產 業 受 到 衝 擊，經 濟 增 長 放 緩，然 而 在 政     negotiations, many industries were hit hard and economic growth
府 一 系 列 振 興 經 濟 的 措 施 之 下，民 生 發 展 相   was sluggish. Nevertheless, upon a series of stimulus measures
對穩定，本集團2019年在行業競爭激烈的情                 rolled out by the government, there was a relatively stable
勢下，堅持穩定通路價格，持續品牌投入，繼                  development in general. Under the intense industry competition
續 推 動 產 品 新 鮮 度 管 理，以 提 供 消 費 者 更 好   in 2019, the Group insisted on maintaining stable prices fixed for
的 產 品 體 驗；在 經 典 品 牌 上 持 續 優 化 品 質，    the sales channels, investing in its brands and facilitating product
新 增 消 費 族 群，在 新 生 活 型 態 的 因 應 上，開     freshness management to provide consumers with a better product
發 新 型 態 產 品，推 升 產 業 升 級，加 強 推 展 新     experience. While the quality of classic brands continued to be
科技、新包裝的運用，自熱科技日臻成熟，自                  improved to attract new consumer groups, novel products were
熱 火 鍋 與 飯 食 的 推 出，擴 大 了 方 便 飲 食 的 疆   developed based on the new lifestyle to facilitate industry upgrade
域，為食品科技發展打開了新的篇章。                     and strengthen the use of new technologies and new packaging.
                                      With more mature self-heating technology, self-heating hotpot and
                                      meals were launched to enlarge the territory of convenient food,
                                      marking a new chapter for the development of food technology.

未來展望                                       PROSPECT

2020年 伊 始，在 新 型 冠 狀 病 毒 COVID-19疫          Since the widespread of the novel coronavirus COVID-19 in
情 的 肆 虐 下，本 集 團 全 力 配 合 政 府 相 關 防 疫        the beginning of 2020, the Group has thoroughly followed the
措 施，並 協 調 各 地 庫 存 及 物 流 情 況，對 重 點          government’s epidemic prevention measures. To pay tribute to
區 域 醫 護 人 員 提 供 免 費 方 便 麵、自 熱 飯 食、         healthcare staff and epidemic prevention personnel, the Group has
飲 料 等 物 資，以 實 質 行 動 向 醫 護 人 員 及 防 疫        also coordinated the inventory level and logistic services in different
工 作 者 致 敬，另 外 本 集 團 積 極 與 地 方 政 府          areas to provide free instant noodles, self-heating rice meals,
溝 通 爭 取 復 工 復 產，制 定 內 部 防 疫 標 準 流 程        beverage and other supplies to healthcare staff in critical regions.
(Standard Operating Procedures)，在全員防       Additionally, the Group has maintained active communications
疫 過 程 中 積 極 投 入 防 疫 及 市 場 民 生 所 需，盡        with local governments to strive for early resumption of work and
早投入生產供應市場民生所需。                             production and formulated internal epidemic prevention standard
                                           operating procedures in order to cater the market and consumer
                                           needs amidst full-scale epidemic prevention by commencing
                                           production and supply as soon as possible.

機 會，本 集 團 將 針 對 此 契 機 做 出 相 應 對 策，         e-commerce and otaku economy. Opportunities are found in time.

管理層討論及分析
Management Discussion & Analysis

經濟環境狀況                                     ECONOMIC ENVIRONMENT

中 華 人 民 共 和 國（「中 國」）2019年 國 內 生 產          The gross domestic product (GDP) of the People’s Republic of
總 值 (GDP)同 比 增 長6.1%，較 去 年 下 降0.5          China (“China” or the “PRC”) recorded a year-on-year increase
個 百 分 點。中 美 貿 易 摩 擦 談 判 延 宕，爭 端 迭          of 6.1% in 2019, down 0.5 percentage points from last year.
起，關稅壁壘等因素影響整體經濟發展，中國                       The China-U.S. trade friction as accompanied by deadlocked
政 府 迅 速 因 應，積 極 推 行 一 系 列 振 新 經 濟 方        negotiations, continuous disputes, tariff barriers and other
案，以減稅降費等措施降低企業負擔、促進民                       situations has affected the overall economic development. The PRC
生 消 費，致2019年 國 民 經 濟 運 行 整 體 平 穩，          government has responded quickly to roll out a series of economic
產 業 結 構 調 整 和 轉 型 升 級 仍 持 續 推 進，內 需        stimulus measures to reduce burden of enterprises and encourage
市場經濟活絡，食品行業依然蓬勃發展。                         consumption such as reduction of taxes and administrative fees.
                                           As a result, the domestic economy remained generally stable in
                                           2019 amidst continuous adjustment, transformation and upgrade
                                           of industrial structure, with active demands in the domestic market
                                           and thriving development of the food industry.


2019年食品行業相關原物料行情走跌，成本                      In 2019, under a wide range of favourable catalysts including lower
壓 力 減 緩，又 適 逢 政 府 推 行 激 勵 經 濟 方 案，         pressure on costs given the reduced prices of raw materials for the
多 項 利 多 刺 激 之 下，眾 廠 家 紛 紛 積 極 搶 市，         food industry as well as economic stimulus from the government,
加 大 市 場 投 入，行 業 競 爭 加 劇，造 成 市 場 通          companies acted promptly to seize market share by increasing
路 價 格 波 動，終 端 行 銷 手 法 花 樣 百 出，各 出          market investment, which resulted in more intense competition
奇 招 爭 取 通 路 及 消 費 者 的 青 睞。市 場 在 生 活        in the industry and fluctuated market prices in channels. A wide
水 平 日 益 提 升、新 科 技 與 新 服 務 日 益 蓬 勃 發        variety of distinctive marketing tactics were adopted to attract
展 之 下，產 品 的 豐 富 性 與 創 新 性 引 領 民 生 消        channels and consumers. Given the improving living standards
費 趨 勢，面 對 時 代 的 變 革，唯 有 重 視 消 費 者          and booming development of new technologies and new services,
需求與滿意度，方能為事業開創新的契機。                        consumption has been led by diverse and innovative products. In
                                           this ever-changing world, new business opportunities can only be
                                           captured by focusing on consumer needs and satisfaction.


新 型 冠 狀 病 毒 肺 炎COVID-19疫 情 於2020年          Since the outbreak of Coronavirus Disease 2019 (“COVID-19”)

甘酒                                    Amazake

甘酒源起於日本，已經有千年的                        Amazake was originated in Japan with a history of over 1,000
歷史，是一種以清酒酒糟為原料                        years. It is a kind of low-alcohol drink made from sake lees. The
調製，酒精含量非常低的飲品。                        “Qian Bai Du Amazake（ 千擺渡甘酒）” newly launched by the
本 集 團2020年1月 全 新 上 市「千                Group in January 2020 uses distillers grains of zake imported from
擺 渡 甘 酒」，選 用 正 宗 日 本 進 口              Japan and high quality. Double fermentation, together with the

財務業績                                       FINANCIAL RESULTS

截 至2019年12月31日 止 年 度（「本 年 度」），             For the year ended 31 December 2019 (the “Year”), the Group

本集團堅持以審慎理財原則的理念為基礎，                        The Group adhered to the principle of financial prudence. It sought
適 度 因 應 市 況 調 節 銷 售 及 市 場 推 廣 開 支、適        to control risk variables and moved forward prudently by moderately

存 在 的 風 險 變 數，紮 實 前 進。本 集 團 的 財 務          conditions, and made appropriate capital expenditures to optimise
部門根據董事會批准及執行董事指導的政策                        and expand its infrastructure. The Group’s finance department
與 程 序 制 訂 財 務 風 險 管 理 政 策，並 由 內 部 稽        formulated financial risk management policies based on the policies
核 部 和 內 審 部 門 定 期 審 查。本 集 團 的 財 務 政        and procedures approved by the Board and guided by executive
策旨在減低利率及匯率波動對本集團整體財                        Directors. These policies were reviewed by the Group’s internal

最低。                                        Group’s financial policy aims at reducing impact of interest rate and

度，本 集 團 於 財 務 風 險 管 理 方 面 繼 續 維 持 審        financial products. The Group continued to adopt a conservative
慎 態 度，並 無 重 大 銀 行 借 貸。由 於 本 集 團 大          approach to financial risk management with no significant bank

轉入其他儲備的公允價值變動產生的累計損                   policies. Such net of tax gain on Disposal together with the
失為人民幣21.4百萬元已於出售後從其他儲                 cumulative loss from fair value changes previously recognised in
備重新分類至保留盈利。                           other comprehensive income and transferred to other reserves of
                                      RMB21.4 million was reclassified from other reserves to retained
                                      earnings upon completion of Disposal.

                                           POLICY

於2019年12月31日，本 集 團，僱 員 總 人 數 為             As at 31 December 2019, the total number of employees of the
29,494人。本 集 團 秉 持 聚 焦 經 營、簡 單 操 作          Group was 29,494. The Group adhered to the policies of focused and
的 原 則，持 續 優 化 組 織 和 人 力 結 構，精 進 企          streamlined operation, and organisational structure and staff structure
業體質，提升效率。在用人策略上，本集團秉                       were improved on a continuous basis to build a stronger and efficient
持 精 兵 簡 政、用 人 唯 才 的 人 資 策 略，堅 持 完          enterprise. In terms of recruitment, the Group was streamlining its
善 的 培 訓、人 文 關 懷 等 機 制，確 保 團 隊 穩 定          administration and progressing towards meritocracy. The Group
及 戰 力 提 升。透 過 內 部 進 階 培 訓、有 計 劃 的          insisted on building a stable and robust team through mechanisms
崗位輪調、外部學習等，不斷提升綜合能力，                       including comprehensive training and humane care. Internal training,
為 集 團 核 心 崗 位 需 求 提 供 保 障，同 時，通 過          regular position transfer, external part-time study and other measures
內 部 拔 擢 為 主、外 部 聘 用 為 輔、績 效 考 核 等          enhanced our overall performance and provided talents for key positions
機 制，確 保 現 有 管 理 團 隊 的 活 力 與 戰 力。為          of the Group steadily. Meanwhile, priorities were given to internal
實 現 年 度 制 定 的 各 項 目 標，本 集 團 設 立 績 效        promotion over external recruitment, and performance appraisal and
獎 金 及 獎 勵 計 劃，嘉 許 及 鼓 勵 於 本 集 團 業 務        other measures were implemented to keep the current management

等目標以及僱員的績效評核發放。                            commend and encourage employees at all levels to make outstanding

        擁有更大的普及範圍及╱或更強大的分            beverages or instant noodles products, have operated their

        需求並影響本集團營運業績。因此，全球           of disposable income, which translated into lower demand
        和地區經濟，包括中國經濟可能持續明            for the Group’s products, affecting the Group’s results of
        顯波動。未來於中國及全球經濟的明顯            operations. As a result, the global and local economies,
        波動或另一次經濟下行可能對中國的食            including the PRC economy, could continue to experience
        品飲料行業及本集團產品的需求造成負            significant volatility. Significant volatility or another downturn

董事之委任及重選                                  Appointment and Re-election of Directors

年。根據企業管治守則，獲委任以填補臨時空                      executive Directors) is three years. In accordance with the
缺或成為董事會新成員之新任董事，須於獲委                      Corporate Governance Code, any new Director appointed to
任後本公司首個股東大會上提呈本公司股東                       fill a casual vacancy or as an addition to the Board shall submit
重選。根據本公司組織章程之規定，於本公司                      himself/herself for re-election by shareholders of the Company at
每屆股東週年大會（「股東週年大會」）上，三                     the first general meeting of the Company after the appointment. In
分之一在任董事（ 或董事並非三或三之倍數，                     accordance with the articles of association of the Company, at each
則為最接近但不少於三分之一之人數）須輪值                      annual general meeting of the Company (the “AGM”), one-third of
退任，而每位董事（ 包括有指定任期之董事）至                    the Directors for the time being or, if their number is not three or
少每三年輪值退任一次。董事的酬金是根據本                      a multiple of three, the number nearest to, but not less than, one-
公司董事提名政策（ 定義見下文）及董事會多                     third, shall retire from office by rotation provided that every Director
元化政策（ 定義見下文）、董事資格、職責、責                    (including those appointed for a specific term) shall be subject to
任及經驗以及當時市況而定。                             retirement by rotation at least once every three years. The Directors’
                                          remunerations are determined with reference to the Director

                                          (as defined below) of the Company, the Directors’ qualifications,

 本公司並無行政總裁，其職責由本集團總經理                     The Company does not have a chief executive officer, whose role
（「總經理」）履行。本公司主席及總經理的職位                    is instead performed by the president of the Group (“President”).
 由不同人士擔任，主席負責確保各董事妥善履                     The roles of the Chairman and the President are performed by

 理層」），管理層由總經理領導。管理層對本集                    authority to carry out day-to-day operations and implementation of
 團的營運向董事會承擔全部責任。                          the strategies and directions set by the Board are delegated to the

董事會成員多元化政策                              BOARD DIVERSITY POLICY

董事會已採納董事會成員多元化政策（ 於2018                 The Board adopted a Board Diversity Policy (as amended on 14
年12月14日修訂，以與企業管治守則的修訂一                  December 2018 to align with the amendments of the Corporate
致，以 及 遵 守 於2019年1月1日 生 效 的 上 市 規        Governance Code and to comply with Rule 13.92 of the Listing
則第13.92條）（「董事會成員多元化政策」），                Rules which came into effect on 1 January 2019) (“Board Diversity
旨在提升董事會效率及維持最高水平之企業                     Policy”), which aims to enhance the effectiveness of the Board and
管治，以及認同並確信董事會成員多元化之裨                    to maintain the highest standards of corporate governance and
益。                                      recognises and embraces the benefits of having a diverse Board.


根據董事會成員多元化政策，為了達到在董事                    Under the Board Diversity Policy, in order to achieve a diversity of
會內有多樣化的觀點、技能及經驗，在決定任                    perspectives, skills and experience within the Board, a number of
何人選加入董事會及繼續委任時將考慮多項                     factors will be considered when deciding on appointments to the
因素，包括但不限於性別、年齡、文化及教育                    Board and the continuation of those appointments, including but
背景、服務年期、技能、地區及行業經驗。在形                   not limited to gender, age, cultural and educational background,
成多樣化的觀點時，本公司亦將計及其本身的                    length of service, skills, regional and industry experience. In forming
業務模式及不時的特定需要。有關董事會成員                    its perspectives on Board diversity, the Company will also take into
多元化政策的詳情，請參閱本公司網站。                      account its own business model and specific needs from time to
                                        time. Please refer to the Company’s website for details of the Board
                                        Diversity Policy.

風險管理及內部監控              RISK MANAGEMENT AND INTERNAL
                       CONTROL

董事會整體負責維持本集團穩健而有效之風    The Board has overall responsibilities for maintaining a sound and
險管理及內部監控系統。該系統包含界定且賦   effective risk management and internal control system of the Group.
予有限度授權的管理架構，旨在協助本集團識   The system includes a defined management structure with limits of
別及管理重大風險，以達成本集團的整體業務   authority, and is designed to help the Group to identify and manage
目標，釐定本集團的風險狀況，監督風險管理   significant risks to achieve the Group’s overall business objectives,
框架結構，審核主要風險及緩解戰略，以及保   determine the Group’s risk profile, oversee risk management
障風險管理的成效及遵守相關法律法規。     framework structure, review key risks and mitigation strategies
                       and to ensure risk management effectiveness and compliance with
                       relevant laws and regulations.


本集團利用自下而上及自上而下的方式，確保   A bottom-up and top-down approach is utilised to ensure a holistic
擁有一套完善的風險管理程序。自下而上的方   risk management process. The bottom-up approach is supported
式以核心業務單位識別風險及確定風險評級    by key business units to identify and prioritise risks while the top-
為基礎；而自上而下的方式則評估及檢討核心   down approach reviews and assesses if risks are comprehensively
業務單位於實現本集團的目標時是否已全面    identified, prioritised, and properly addressed by key business units
識別風險、為風險劃分級別及予以妥善處理。   to accomplish the Group’s objectives. The management team is
管理層團隊為持續評估風險定期監測新出現    regularly monitoring emerging risks for continuous risk assessment
的風險，建立以風險管理為基礎的內部審核計   purpose and for building the risk-management based internal audit
劃，並以現時的風險擔保為目的週期性評估風   plan and periodically assess the adequacy and effectiveness of
險管理及內部控制的適當性及有效性。此舉提   risk management and internal controls for ongoing risk assurance
高了本集團風險及控制框架的有效性。      purposes. This enhances the effectiveness of the Group’s risk and
                       control framework.


本集團設立風險管理及內部監控系統，旨在減   The Group’s risk management and internal control system is
低風險、保障本集團資產、防止及偵查欺詐及   designed to reduce risks, safeguard the Group’s assets, prevent
保障股東投資，確保維持適當會計記錄，遵守   and detect fraud and protect shareholders’ investment as well
合適法規及規例。於本年度，董事會透過與管   as to ensure that proper accounting records are maintained and
理層、內部審計團隊及外聘核數師進行討論的   applicable legislation and regulations are complied with. During the
方式，以及內部審計團隊所呈交報告，從不同   Year, through discussions with the management, the internal audit
角度評估與檢討本集團風險管理及內部監控    team and external auditors and with reference to a report submitted
系統之有效性，其中包括財務監控、營運與合   by the internal audit team, the Board has conducted assessments
規監控以及風險管理。風險管理及內部控制系   and reviews of the effectiveness of the Group’s risk management
統每年由董事會審核且每次審核覆蓋整個財    and internal control system in various perspectives, including,
政年度。                   among others, financial control, operational and compliance
                       controls and risk management. The risk management and internal
                       control systems are reviewed by the Board annually and each
                       review covers a whole financial year.

的鑒證結論。                                assurance conclusion thereon.

任 是 閱 讀 其 他 信 息，在 此 過 程 中，考 慮 其 他     statements, our responsibility is to read the other information and,

                                           STATEMENTS

我 們 的 目 標，是 對 綜 合 財 務 報 表 整 體 是 否 不        Our objectives are to obtain reasonable assurance about whether
存在由於欺詐或錯誤而導致的重大錯誤陳述                        the consolidated financial statements as a whole are free from
取 得 合 理 保 證，並 出 具 包 括 我 們 意 見 的 核 數        material misstatement, whether due to fraud or error, and to issue
師報告。我們僅向         閣下（ 作為整體）報告，              an auditor’s report that includes our opinion. We report our opinion
除 此 之 外 本 報 告 別 無 其 他 目 的。我 們 不 會 就        solely to you, as a body, and for no other purpose. We do not
本報告的內容向任何其他人士負上或承擔任                        assume responsibility towards or accept liability to any other person
何 責 任。合 理 保 證 是 高 水 平 的 保 證，但 不 能          for the contents of this report. Reasonable assurance is a high level
保 證 按 照《香 港 審 計 準 則》進 行 的 審 計，在            of assurance, but is not a guarantee that an audit conducted in
某 一 重 大 錯 誤 陳 述 存 在 時 總 能 發 現。錯 誤 陳        accordance with HKSAs will always detect a material misstatement
述 可 以 由 欺 詐 或 錯 誤 引 起，如 果 合 理 預 期 它        when it exists. Misstatements can arise from fraud or error and are
們單獨或匯總起來可能影響綜合財務報表使                        considered material if, individually or in the aggregate, they could
用者依賴綜合財務報表所作出的經濟決定，                        reasonably be expected to influence the economic decisions of
則有關的錯誤陳述可被視作重大。                            users taken on the basis of these consolidated financial statements.

    2.10 非金融資產減值                       2.10 Impairment of non-financial assets

      無 確 定 可 使 用 年 期 之 資 產（ 如 商           Assets that have an indefinite useful life – for example,
      譽或未能使用之無形資產）毋須攤                      goodwill or intangible assets not ready to use – are
      銷，而會每年進行減值測試。須予                      not subject to amortisation and are tested annually for
      攤銷之資產當出現事宜或事態變                       impairment. Assets that are subject to amortisation are
      動顯示未必能收回賬面值時檢討                       reviewed for impairment whenever events or changes
      是 否 減 值。減 值 虧 損 按 資 產 賬 面            in circumstances indicate that the carrying amount may
      值 超 過 可 收 回 數 額 之 差 額 確 認。           not be recoverable. An impairment loss is recognised
      可收回數額為資產公平值減出售                       for the amount by which the asset’s carrying amount
      成 本 及 使 用 價 值 兩 者 之 較 高 者。           exceeds its recoverable amount. The recoverable
      就評估減值而言，資產按可獨立識                      amount is the higher of an asset’s fair value less costs
      別 現 金 流 量 之 最 低 水 準（ 現 金 產           to sell and value in use. For the purposes of assessing
      生單位）分類。出現減值虧損之非                      impairment, assets are grouped at the lowest levels
      金融資產（ 商譽除外）則於每個報                     for which there are separately identifiable cash flows
      告日期就其減值之撥回可能性進                       (cash-generating units). Non-financial assets other than
      行檢討。                                 goodwill that suffered an impairment are reviewed for
                                           possible reversal of the impairment at each reporting
                                           date.

    2.13 抵銷金融工具                                2.13 Offsetting financial instruments

       金 融 資 產 及 負 債 會 作 抵 銷，而 於                        Financial assets and liabilities are offset and the net
       有法律上可強制執行權利抵銷已                                   amount reported in the balance sheet when there is
       確認金額及有意以淨額基準清償                                   a legally enforceable right to offset the recognised
       或 同 時 變 現 資 產 及 清 償 負 債 時，                       amounts and there is an intention to settle on a
       在資產負債表列報之淨額。法律上                                  net basis or realise the asset and settle the liability
       可強制執行權利不得為視乎日後                                   simultaneously. The legally enforceable right must not
       事件而定及必須為正常業務中及                                   be contingent on future events and must be enforceable
       於公司或對手方拖欠款項、無力償                                  in the normal course of business and in the event of
       債時或破產時可強制執行。                                     default, insolvency or bankruptcy of the company or the
                                                        counterparty.


    2.14 存貨                                    2.14 Inventories

       原材料、直接勞工及其他直接成本                                  in progress comprises raw materials, direct labour, other
       及 相 關 生 產 經 常 開 支（ 按 日 常 運                       direct costs and related production overheads (based
       營 能 力 計），不 包 括 借 貸 成 本。可                         on normal operating capacity). It excludes borrowing
       變現淨值是以日常業務之估計售                                   costs. Net realisable value is the estimated selling price
       價減去適用之變動銷售開支後所                                   in the ordinary course of business, less applicable
       得之數額。                                            variable selling expenses.

    2.23 撥備                                    2.23 Provisions

       倘本集團需就過去事項承擔現有                                   Provisions are recognised when the Group has a
       法律或推定責任，而有可能導致資                                  present legal or constructive obligation as a result of
       源流出以履行該責任，並能可靠估                                  past events; it is probable that an outflow of resources
       計金額才會確認撥備。並無就未來                                  will be required to settle the obligation; and the
       經營虧損確認撥備。                                        amount has been reliably estimated. Provisions are not
                                                        recognised for future operating losses.


       倘出現多項類似債務，會否導致經                                  Where there are a number of similar obligations, the
       濟利益流出以清償債務乃經考慮                                   likelihood that an outflow will be required in settlement is
       債務之整體類別後確定。即使同類                                  determined by considering the class of obligations as a
       別債務中任何一項可能流出經濟                                   whole. A provision is recognised even if the likelihood of
       利益之機會不大，仍會確認撥備。                                  an outflow with respect to any one item included in the
                                                        same class of obligations may be small.


       撥備乃按清償債項預期所需開支                                   Provisions are measured at the present value of the
       之現值，使用反映市場目前所評估                                  expenditures expected to be required to settle the
       之 金 錢 時 間 價 值，以 及 該 項 債 項                        obligation using a pre-tax rate that reflects current
       特 定 之 風 險 之 除 稅 前 稅 率 計 算。                       market assessments of the time value of money and
       因時間的流逝導致的撥備增加會                                   the risks specific to the obligation. The increase in
       確認為利息開支。                                         the provision due to passage of time is recognised as
                                                        interest expense.

       後，貨品交付方告完成。                          with the sales contract, the acceptance provisions have

                                            criteria for acceptance have been satisfied.


       產品通常以一段時期內的銷售總                       The products are often sold with retrospective volume

       售。該等銷售的收益乃基於合約規                      time. Revenue from these sales is recognised based on

       及提供折扣，且收益僅於重大撥回                      estimate and provide for the discounts, and revenue is

       時 確 認 退 款 責 任（ 包 括 在 其 他 應           (included in other payables and accruals) is recognised
       付賬款及應計費用內）。由於銷售                      for expected volume discounts payable to customers
       之 信 貸 期 為60至90日，符 合 市 場              in relation to sales. No significant element of financing
       慣例，故並不存在融資因素。                        is deemed present as the sales are made with a credit

    2.28 或然負債及或然資產                             2.28 Contingent liabilities and contingent assets

       或然負債乃一項因過往事件可能                                   A contingent liability is a possible obligation that arises
       產生的承擔，而該等過往事件的存                                  from past events and whose existence will only be
       在僅可由一項或多項並非由本集                                   confirmed by the occurrence or non-occurrence of one
       團全權控制的日後不明朗事件的                                   or more uncertain future events not wholly within the
       存在與否確定。或然負債亦可為一                                  control of the Group. It can also be a present obligation
       項因不大可能需要耗用經濟資源                                   arising from past events that is not recognised because
       或承擔之金額未能可靠地計算而                                   it is not probable that outflow of economic resources
       未確認之過往事件產生之現有承                                   will be required or the amount of obligation cannot be
       擔。                                               measured reliably.


       或然負債未予以確認，惟已於財務                                  A contingent liability is not recognised but is disclosed
       報表中披露。倘耗用經濟資源之可                                  in the financial statements. When a change in the
       能性出現變動致使有可能需耗用                                   probability of an outflow occurs so that outflow is
       經 濟 資 源，則 或 然 負 債 將 確 認 為                        probable, it will then be recognised as a provision.
       撥備。


       或然資產指因為已發生的事件而                                   A contingent asset is a possible asset that arises from
       可能產生的資產，此等資產只能根                                  past events and whose existence will be confirmed only
       據本集團不能完全控制的一項或                                   by the occurrence or non-occurrence of one or more
       多項不確定事件的出現與否方能                                   uncertain events not wholly within the control of the
       確認。                                              Group.

      集團繼續以持續經營方式為股東            safeguard the Group’s ability to continue as a going
      提供回報以及為其他權益持有人            concern in order to provide returns for shareholders
      帶來利益，並且維持最佳資本結構           and benefits for other stakeholders and to maintain an
      以減少資金成本。                  optimal capital structure to reduce the cost of capital.


      為維持或調整資本結構，本集團可           In order to maintain or adjust the capital structure, the
      調整向股東支付股息之金額、發行           Group may adjust the amount of dividends paid to
      新股份或出售資產以減少債項。            shareholders, issue new shares or sell assets to reduce
                                debt.


      與行業其他公司一樣，本集團根據           Consistent with others in the industry, the Group
      資本負債比率監控其資本。資本負           monitors capital on the basis of the gearing ratio. This
      債比率乃按債項淨額除以總資本            ratio is calculated as net debt divided by total capital.
      計算。

      試 中 的 現 值 淨 額 造 成 重 大 影 響，                       rate assumptions in the cash flow projections, could
      並因而對本集團之財務狀況及經                                   materially affect the net present value in the impairment
      營業績造成影響。倘預測業績及由                                  test and as a result affect the Group’s financial position
      此得出的未來現金流量預測有重                                   and results of operations. If there is a significant adverse
      大不利變動，則可能需於綜合全面                                  change in the projected performance and resulting
      收益表中扣除減值金額。                                      future cash flow projections, it may be necessary to take
                                                       an impairment charge to the consolidated statement of
                                                       comprehensive income.

15 租賃（ 包括土地使用權）                  15 LEASES (INCLUDING LAND USE RIGHTS)
  （ 續）                              (CONTINUED)

  (iii) 本集團之租賃活動及有關活動               (iii) The Group’s leasing activities and how they
        之入賬方式                             are accounted for

    本集團之土地使用權全部均已悉                      The Group’s land use rights are all fully prepaid.
    數預付。此外，本集團租賃多個辦                     In addition, the Group leases various offices and
    公室及倉庫。租約一般以固定期限                     warehouses. Rental contracts are typically made for
    三個月至十年訂立，惟可能涵蓋如                     fixed periods of 3 months to 10 years, but may have
    下文(iv)所述延期選擇權。                      extension options as described in (iv) below.


    租賃期按個別情況磋商，並包含各                     Lease terms are negotiated on an individual basis and
    種 不 同 條 款 及 條 件。除 出 租 人 持           contain a wide range of different terms and conditions.
    有 之 租 賃 資 產 中 的 抵 押 權 益 外，          The lease agreements do not impose any covenants
    租賃協議並無施加任何限制，惟租                     other than the security interests in the leased assets
    賃資產不得用作借貸的抵押。                       that are held by the lessor. Leased assets may not be
                                        used as security for borrowing purposes.


  (iv) 延期及終止選擇權                     (iv) Extension and termination options

    本集團的若干物業及設備租賃包                      Extension and termination options are included in a
    含 延 期 及 終 止 選 擇 權。該 等 條 款           number of property and equipment leases across the
    乃用於就管理合約令經營靈活性                      Group. These terms are used to maximise operational
    最 大 化。所 持 有 的 大 部 分 延 期 及           flexibility in terms of managing contracts. The majority of
    終 止 選 擇 權 僅 可 由 本 集 團 行 使，          extension and termination options held are exercisable
    惟不得由有關出租人行使。                        only by the Group and not by the respective lessor.

27 其他儲備（ 續）                          27 OTHER RESERVES (CONTINUED)

  (a)   繳入盈餘                            (a)   Contribution surplus

        本公司的繳入盈餘主要指本公司                        Contribution surplus of the Company mainly represent
        股份上市之前本公司權益持有人                        the net balance of contributions from and distributions
        根據就上市已進行之重組所作出                        to the equity holder of the Company prior to the
        之注資及向彼等作出分派的結餘                        listing of shares of the Company pursuant to the
        淨額。                                   reorganisation for the purpose of the Listing.


        按照開曼群島法律與法規，該等繳                       According to the law and regulation of the Cayman
        入盈餘可分派予本公司的權益持                        Islands, such contribution surplus is distributable to the
        有人。                                   equity holders of the Company.


  (b)   法定儲備                            (b)   Statutory reserves

        中國法律與法規要求在中國註冊                        The PRC laws and regulations require companies
        的公司，在對權益持有人作溢利分                       registered in the PRC to provide for certain statutory
        派前從各自的法定財務報表所申                        reserves, which are appropriated from the net profit
        報 的 溢 利 淨 額（ 在 抵 銷 了 以 往 年            as reported in their respective statutory financial
        度的累計虧損後）中提撥一定的法                       statements after offsetting accumulated losses from
        定儲備。所有法定儲備乃就特定目                       prior years and before profit distributions to equity
        的而增設。                                 holder. All statutory reserves are created for specific
                                              purposes.


        作為外商獨資企業和內資企業註                        PRC subsidiaries incorporated as wholly-foreign
        冊成立的中國附屬公司，在進行當                       owned enterprises and domestic companies are
        年度除稅後溢利分派時，須將法定                       required to appropriate 10% of statutory net profits to
        溢 利 淨 額 之10%撥 作 法 定 盈 餘 公             statutory surplus reserves, upon distribution of their
        積金，直至法定盈餘公積金不少於                       post-tax profits of the current year, until the statutory
        公司之註冊資本50%為止。此外，                      surplus reserve is not less than 50% of its registered
        此等公司可在各自董事會酌情決                        capital. In addition, at the discretion of the respective
        定下將部份除稅後溢利撥入職工                        boards of directors, these companies may allocate
        福利及花紅儲備及任意盈餘公積                        a portion of their post-tax profits to the staff welfare
        金。作為中外合資經營企業註冊成                       and bonus reserve and discretionary surplus reserve.
        立的中國附屬公司，在其各自董事                       PRC subsidiaries incorporated as sino-foreign equity
        會酌情決定下將部份法定除稅後                        joint venture companies may allocate a portion of their
        溢利撥入法定儲備。                             statutory post-tax profits to the statutory reserves at the
                                              discretion of the respective boards of directors.


        法定盈餘公積金只能用於彌補公                        The statutory surplus reserves shall only be used to
        司的虧損、擴大公司的生產經營或                       make up losses of the companies, to expand the
        增加公司資本。                               companies’ production operations, or to increase the
                                              capital of the companies.

37 報告期後發生的事件                                              37 EVENTS OCCURRING AFTER THE
                                                             REPORTING PERIOD
  於2020年 年 初，新 型 冠 狀 病 毒 肺 炎 疫                                After the outbreak of Coronavirus Disease 2019 (“COVID-19
  情 在 全 國 爆 發（ 以 下 簡 稱「新 冠 肺 炎 爆                              outbreak”) in early 2020, a series of precautionary and control
  發」），對疫情的防控工作正在全國範圍                                          measures have been and continued to be implemented
  內持續進行，全國範圍延長春節假期，                                           across the PRC including, but not limited to, extension of
  部分地區春節後延遲復工，對人員流動                                           the Chinese New Year holiday nationwide, postponement of
  和交通進行一定程度的控制，對特定人                                           work resumption after the Chinese New Year holiday in some
  群進行隔離，提高工廠及辦公場所的衛                                           regions, certain level of restrictions and controls over people
  生和防疫要求，以及一系列增加社會距                                           travelling and traffic arrangements, quarantine of certain
  離的措施等。這些措施對本集團部分地                                           residents, heightening of hygiene and epidemic prevention
  區的運輸和生產成本有一定短期影響，                                           requirements in factories and offices and encouraged social
  但不重大。於本財務報表獲授權刊發日                                           distancing, etc. It may have a temporary impact on the
  期，本集團尚未發現新冠肺炎爆發對本                                           Group’s transportation and production cost in some regions,
  集團的財務報表有重大影響。                                               but not to the extent of material. As at the date on which

                                                              Group was not aware of any material effect on the financial
